\u2013 INFORMATION WE COLLECT</span></strong></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">When you purchase something from our store, as part of the buying and selling process, we collect the personal information you give us (known in this policy as Personal Information) such as your name, mailing address, telephone number, mobile phone number, email address, and other identification and contact information. It may also include certain financial information that we collect. With this information, we can fill your service and product orders and better provide you with personalized services.&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">When you browse our store, we also automatically receive your computer\u2019s internet protocol (IP) address in order to provide us with information that helps us learn about your browser and operating system.</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">Email marketing (if applicable): With your permission, we may send you emails about our shop, new products and other updates.&nbsp;</span></p><p><br /><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\"><!--[if !supportLineBreakNewLine]--><br /><!--[endif]--></span></p><p><strong><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">SECTION 2 - CONSENT</span></strong></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.0pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><em><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">How do we get your consent?</span></em></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">When you provide us with Personal Information to complete a transaction, verify your credit card, place an order, arrange for a delivery or return a purchase, we infer that you consent to our collecting it and using it for that specific reason only.</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">If we ask for your Personal Information for a secondary reason, like marketing, we will either ask you directly for your expressed consent, or provide you with an opportunity to say no.&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.0pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><em><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">How do you withdraw your consent?</span></em></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">If after you opt-in, you change your mind, you may withdrawing your consent for us to contact you, for the continued collection, use or disclosure of your information, at anytime, by contacting us at hello@Studio-McGee.com.&nbsp;</span></p><p><br /><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\"><!--[if !supportLineBreakNewLine]--><br /><!--[endif]--></span></p><p><strong><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">SECTION 3 \u2013 INFORMATION WE SHARE WITH OTHERS</span></strong></p><p><strong><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">&nbsp;</span></strong></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">We contract with other companies (such as shippers, credit card processors and website developers) to provide certain services to Studio McGee to fulfill orders, provide contests and create our website. In some instances, these contractors may have access to Personally Information but they are contractually obligated to maintain the confidentiality of the information and to use it only for purposes of carrying out the business of Studio McGee.</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">These companies are prohibited by contract from using this information for their own marketing purposes or from sharing this information with anyone other than Studio McGee.</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">Your Personally Information is never shared with any third party (except as described above to our contractors who use it ONLY to carry out Studio McGee business.)</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><strong><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">SECTION 4 - DISCLOSURE</span></strong></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.0pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">Notwithstanding the above policies, we may disclose your personally information to appropriate third parties if we are required to do so by law or we believe that such action is necessary:</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">To comply with legal process such as a search warrant, subpoena or court order;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">To protect the company\u2019s rights and property;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">To investigate reports of fraud or of users sending material using a false e-mail address or users sending harassing, threatening, or abusive messages;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">To protect against misuse or unauthorized use of our website; or</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">During emergencies, such as when we believe someone\u2019s physical safety is at risk.</span></p><p><br /><strong><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">SECTION 5 \u2013 </span></strong><strong><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">Squarespace.com</span></strong></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.0pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">Our store is hosted on </span><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">Squarespace.com.</span><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\"> </span><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">They provide us with the online e-commerce platform that allows us to sell our products and services to you.&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">Your data is stored through </span><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">Squarespace.com</span><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\"> data storage, databases and the general </span><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">Squarespace.com</span><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\"> application. They store your data on a secure server behind a firewall.</span></p><p><em><span style=\"font-size:10.0pt\">&nbsp;</span></em></p><p><em><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">Payment:</span></em></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">If you choose a direct payment gateway to complete your purchase, then </span><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">Squarespace.com</span><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\"> </span><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">stores your credit card data. It is encrypted through the Payment Card Industry Data Security Standard (PCI-DSS). Your purchase transaction data is stored only as long as is necessary to complete your purchase transaction. After that is complete, your purchase transaction information is deleted.</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.0pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">All direct payment gateways adhere to the standards set by PCI-DSS as managed by the PCI Security Standards Council, which is a joint effort of brands like Visa, MasterCard, American Express and Discover.&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.0pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">PCI-DSS requirements help ensure the secure handling of credit card information by our store and its service providers.</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">For more insight, you may also want to read </span><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">Squarespace.com</span><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\"> Terms of Service here or Privacy Statement here.</span></p><p><br /><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\"><!--[if !supportLineBreakNewLine]--><br /><!--[endif]--></span></p><p><strong><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">SECTION 6 - THIRD-PARTY SERVICES</span></strong></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.0pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">Studio McGee employs other companies and individuals to perform functions on our behalf, such as fulfilling orders, delivering packages, sending postal mail and e-mail, providing search results and links, and processing credit card payments. These third-party service providers have access to Personal Information needed to perform their functions but may not use it for any other purpose.</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">Certain third-party service providers, such as payment gateways and other payment transaction processors, have their own privacy policies in respect to the information we are required to provide to them for your purchase-related transactions.</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">For these providers, we recommend that you read their privacy policies so you can understand the manner in which your personal information will be handled by these providers. &nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.0pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">Once you leave our store\u2019s website or are redirected to a third-party website or application, you are no longer governed by this Privacy Policy or our website\u2019s Terms of Service.&nbsp;</span></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.0pt\">&nbsp;</span></p><p><em><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">Links:</span></em></p><p><span style=\"font-size:10.5pt\">When you click on links on our store, they may direct you away from our site.
